- **performance-engineer**: Performance & scalability engineer for Bodhi. Use before a client demo or release, after changes to retrieval, ingestion, the agent graph, DB queries or streaming, or when someone says "it feels slow" / "will it scale?". Writes and runs load tests (Locust) against the fake-LLM profile, measures latency against the budgets in docs/SPEC.md, profiles bottlenecks and recommends measured fixes. Does not change application code. ([chntraining/ragrepos](https://github.com/chntraining/ragrepos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/performance-engineer.md))

- **performance-engineer**: The speed voice on a system or change — finds where the time actually goes, and refuses to optimize on a hunch. Asks what the budget is, what the marginal cost per unit is, whether the measurement was taken on a quiet machine, and whether the fix survives contact with the profile. Distrusts its own instinct for interesting optimizations, because the expensive thing is usually structural — allocation, contention, redundant setup, a data structure chosen for the wrong access pattern — and almost never the algorithm everyone is looking at. Can profile and diagnose OR implement the optimization.… ([dpep/claude](https://github.com/dpep/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/personas/agents/performance-engineer.md))

Language-agnostic — works with any smart contract language (Solidity, Rust, Go, Move, Cairo, Vyper). Spawned by multi-persona-orchestrator. Applies Feynman questioning at every stack depth. ([calc1f4r/Horus](https://github.com/calc1f4r/Horus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/persona-dfs.md) · 2 copies)
- **persona-dhh**: Avatar of David Heinemeier Hansson (dhh) for simulated debates and meetings. Creator of Ruby on Rails, partner at 37signals, professional racing driver; pugnacious, declarative, contrarian-by-default debate style. ([zirubak/persona-studio](https://github.com/zirubak/persona-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-dhh.md) · ★8)
- **persona-discovery**: Derive the real end-user roles of an application from code evidence and emit persona specs. Use before any exploratory or role-based testing, when onboarding to an unfamiliar codebase, or when asked who the users of a system are. Do NOT use to test behavior — this agent only identifies and specifies personas. Loads the engineering-integrity and project-memory skills. ([AlexanderVegazo26/agents](https://github.com/AlexanderVegazo26/agents/blob/HEAD/commandcode-suite/agents/persona-discovery.md) · 4 copies)
- **persona-doc-author**: Draft a per-role behavioral persona doc (canonical persona template) — a domain-specific behavioral persona anchored on what the person thinks in (mental unit), the failure they cannot absorb, how they think, what they see vs don't, their hand-offs, and their own words. Filesystem-only. Unlike story/journey authors it returns the WHOLE file (front-matter + body) — a persona carries no Linear-children fields, so there is no deterministic builder step; only `last_reviewed` is dispatcher-stamped. ([Brite-Nites/brite-claude-plugins](https://github.com/Brite-Nites/brite-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/flow-architecture/agents/persona-doc-author.md))
- **persona-domain-expert**: PERSONA TYPE: Contributing (default) — no extra frontmatter fields needed. Contributing personas inject observations into existing primary persona confirmations. This is the right choice for most custom personas. ([isdlc/isdlc-knowledge-service](https://github.com/isdlc/isdlc-knowledge-service/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/persona-domain-expert.md))
- **persona-driver**: Isolated experiential UX driver for one persona × surface slice. Spawn this agent from /ux-review:run when doing a broad or exhaustive review — browser snapshots are large and clutter the main context, so each slice runs in its own agent. Pass the app URL, the persona (who they are + their jobs-to-be-done), the surface/area to cover, the viewports and states to exercise, and the product's stated promises (the yardstick). The agent drives a real browser via Playwright MCP, works the review lenses on that slice, screenshots every defect, watches the console/network for silent failures, and retu… ([AlphaBravoCompany/codsworth-marketplace](https://github.com/AlphaBravoCompany/codsworth-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/ux-review/agents/persona-driver.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **persona-execution-coach**: Execution Coach advisor for Personal Board of Advisors. Provides perspective on action, discipline, habits, and turning plans into results. ([doriancollier/lifeos-starter](https://github.com/doriancollier/lifeos-starter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/persona-execution-coach.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **persona-expert**: レビュー対象の属性に合う職能の専門家として、文脈を知らされずに成果物を体験し、所感・理解・つまずき・ネガ・ポジと専門的な誤りの指摘を返す。fresh-eyes:review から起動される。 ([andy0911/fresh_eyes](https://github.com/andy0911/fresh_eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-expert.md))

- **persona-extractor**: 你是Persona提取专家。根据用户提供的输入，提取三层Persona档案：Identity（基础人格）、Expression（表达风格）、Memory（记忆种子）。 ([liaozicen666-tech/AI-podcast-dual-host-mandarin](https://github.com/liaozicen666-tech/AI-podcast-dual-host-mandarin/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-extractor.md))

- **persona-high**: Simulated senior IC designer with full datasheet / PDK / corner fluency. Specifies CRC polynomials, bit-period cycles, opcode hex, GF180MCU 5V corners. Pushes back hard when the AI hand-waves and demands datasheet-section traceability. - **persona-optimist**: [EXPERIMENTAL — "Framework plus"] Use this agent to add a creative, divergent, opportunity-seeking lens to a Where-to-Play discussion — Worksheet 1 idea generation or a Worksheet 2 scoring debate. It argues the optimistic, expansive case: bigger markets, bolder re-linking, upside others miss. It is one voice in a debate, never the decider, and is paired with persona-skeptic for balance. Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: The team's opportunity set feels timid. user: "Push us — what bold markets are we dismissing too fast?" assistant: "I'll bring in the persona-optimist agent for the expansive, opti… ([codeeater/where-to-plai-plugin](https://github.com/codeeater/where-to-plai-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/where-to-plai/agents/persona-optimist.md))
- **persona-orchestrator**: Three-persona evaluation agent that scores the codebase from Technical, Human, and Meta perspectives — then synthesizes a holistic verdict using weighted averaging. ([ShaheerKhawaja/ProductionOS](https://github.com/ShaheerKhawaja/ProductionOS/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-orchestrator.md) · ★8)
- **persona-orchestrator-engineering**: Use for Engineering work — architecture, refactor, migration, api, schema, database, performance, latency, scalability, reliability. Runs a persona panel end to end: names the end customer and the decision first, selects personas, adjudicates findings against the artifact, and returns recommendations each addressed to a named consumer. Example: Review an API contract change before consumers depend on it. Not for work that belongs to another discipline — run `persona orchestrate "&lt;task&gt;" --json` and follow the lane it resolves. ([tyroneross/ai-persona-lab](https://github.com/tyroneross/ai-persona-lab/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-orchestrator-engineering.md))
- **persona-orchestrator-marketing**: Use for Marketing work — marketing, messaging, copy, landing page, campaign, launch announcement, brand, content, seo, audience. Runs a persona panel end to end: names the end customer and the decision first, selects personas, adjudicates findings against the artifact, and returns recommendations each addressed to a named consumer. Example: Judge whether a landing page explains the offer to someone who has never heard of it. Not for work that belongs to another discipline — run `persona orchestrate "&lt;task&gt;" --json` and follow the lane it resolves. ([tyroneross/ai-persona-lab](https://github.com/tyroneross/ai-persona-lab/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-orchestrator-marketing.md))
- **persona-orchestrator-product**: Use for Product-manager work — product, roadmap, prioritize, prioritization, feature, prd, spec, scope, backlog, launch. Runs a persona panel end to end: names the end customer and the decision first, selects personas, adjudicates findings against the artifact, and returns recommendations each addressed to a named consumer. Example: Decide whether to build a requested feature or solve the need another way. Not for work that belongs to another discipline — run `persona orchestrate "&lt;task&gt;" --json` and follow the lane it resolves. ([tyroneross/ai-persona-lab](https://github.com/tyroneross/ai-persona-lab/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-orchestrator-product.md))
- **persona-orchestrator-strategy**: Use for Strategist work — strategy, strategic, business model, market, competition, competitive, competitor, positioning, moat, defensibility. Runs a persona panel end to end: names the end customer and the decision first, selects personas, adjudicates findings against the artifact, and returns recommendations each addressed to a named consumer. Example: Judge whether entering an adjacent market strengthens or dilutes the core business. Not for work that belongs to another discipline — run `persona orchestrate "&lt;task&gt;" --json` and follow the lane it resolves. ([tyroneross/ai-persona-lab](https://github.com/tyroneross/ai-persona-lab/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-orchestrator-strategy.md))
- **persona-orchestrator-ui-ux**: Use for UI/UX design work — ui, ux, interface, screen, flow, onboarding, dashboard, component, layout, navigation. Runs a persona panel end to end: names the end customer and the decision first, selects personas, adjudicates findings against the artifact, and returns recommendations each addressed to a named consumer. Example: Review a redesigned settings screen before it ships. Not for work that belongs to another discipline — run `persona orchestrate "&lt;task&gt;" --json` and follow the lane it resolves. ([tyroneross/ai-persona-lab](https://github.com/tyroneross/ai-persona-lab/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-orchestrator-ui-ux.md))
- **persona-panel**: Nemotron-Personas-Korea 기반 합성 시민 패널 시뮬레이션. Use when: 정책/조례/예산안을 다양한 시민 관점에서 검토, 찬반 논거 다각화, 정치적 중립성 검증. ([luncliff/politics-agents](https://github.com/luncliff/politics-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/persona-panel.md) · ★1)
- **persona-panel-orchestrator**: The general persona-panel orchestrator and the router to the lane-specific ones. Use when a review, panel, council or roster is requested and the discipline is not already settled — it resolves the lane (UI/UX design, product, strategy, engineering, marketing) and hands off, or runs the panel itself when the work spans disciplines or the host has no subagents. Runs the full loop: name the end customer and the decision, select personas, dispatch independent blind passes, adjudicate findings against the artifact, synthesize preserving conflicts, and return recommendations each addressed to a na… ([tyroneross/ai-persona-lab](https://github.com/tyroneross/ai-persona-lab/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-panel-orchestrator.md))
